    ["!"]=Enum.KeyCode.One,["@"]=Enum.KeyCode.Two,["$"]=Enum.KeyCode.Four,
    ["%"]=Enum.KeyCode.Five,["^"]=Enum.KeyCode.Six,["*"]=Enum.KeyCode.Eight,["("]=Enum.KeyCode.Nine,
    ["Q"]=Enum.KeyCode.Q,["W"]=Enum.KeyCode.W,["E"]=Enum.KeyCode.E,["T"]=Enum.KeyCode.T,
    ["Y"]=Enum.KeyCode.Y,["I"]=Enum.KeyCode.I,["O"]=Enum.KeyCode.O,["P"]=Enum.KeyCode.P,
    ["S"]=Enum.KeyCode.S,["D"]=Enum.KeyCode.D,["G"]=Enum.KeyCode.G,["H"]=Enum.KeyCode.H,
    ["J"]=Enum.KeyCode.J,["L"]=Enum.KeyCode.L,["Z"]=Enum.KeyCode.Z,["C"]=Enum.KeyCode.C,
    ["V"]=Enum.KeyCode.V,["B"]=Enum.KeyCode.B
}

local function pianoIsShift(k)
    return k:match("^[A-Z]$") or k:match("^[!@%$%^%*(]$")
end

local function pianoPlayChord(keys)
    local un, sh = {}, {}
    for _, k in ipairs(keys) do
        local c = pianoKeyMap[k]
        if c then
            if pianoIsShift(k) then table.insert(sh, c) else table.insert(un, c) end
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        if #un > 0 then
            for _, c in ipairs(un) do pianoVim:SendKeyEvent(true, c, false, game) end
            task.wait(0.02)
            for _, c in ipairs(un) do pianoVim:SendKeyEvent(false, c, false, game) end
        end
        if #sh > 0 then
            pianoVim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, false, game)
            task.wait(0.01)
            for _, c in ipairs(sh) do pianoVim:SendKeyEvent(true, c, false, game) end
            task.wait(0.02)
            for _, c in ipairs(sh) do pianoVim:SendKeyEvent(false, c, false, game) end
            pianoVim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift, false, game)
        end
    end)
end

local function pianoScanSongs()
    local names, map = {}, {}
    for _, p in ipairs(listfiles(_G.PianoState.PianoSongsPath)) do
        if p:lower():sub(-4) == ".mid" then
            local n = p:match("([^/\\]+)$")
            if n then
                table.insert(names, n)
                map[n] = p
            end
        end
    end
    if #names == 0 then table.insert(names, "No songs found") end
    table.sort(names)
    return names, map
end

local function pianoStartPlayback()
    if _G.PianoState.PlaybackConn then
        _G.PianoState.PlaybackConn:Disconnect()
        _G.PianoState.PlaybackConn = nil
    end
    _G.PianoState.PlaybackConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if _G.PianoState.Status ~= "Playing" then return end
        local song = _G.PianoState.Song
        local idx = _G.PianoState.Index
        if idx > #song then
            if _G.PianoState.Looping then
                _G.PianoState.Index = 1
                _G.PianoState.LastTime = 0
                _G.PianoState.CurrentTime = 0
            else
                _G.PianoState.Status = "Stopped"
                _G.PianoState.Index = 1
                _G.PianoState.LastTime = 0
                _G.PianoState.CurrentTime = 0
            end
            return
        end
        local e = song[idx]
        local w = (e.time - _G.PianoState.LastTime) / _G.PianoState.Speed
        if w > 0.001 then return end
        local window = math.max(0.01, 0.02 / _G.PianoState.Speed)
        local chord, j = {}, idx
        while j <= #song and (song[j].time - e.time) <= window do
            local note = song[j].note + _G.PianoState.Transpose
            local k = pianoNoteMap[note]
            if k then table.insert(chord, k) end
            j = j + 1
        end
        if #chord > 0 then pianoPlayChord(chord) end
        _G.PianoState.LastTime = e.time
        _G.PianoState.Index = j
        _G.PianoState.CurrentTime = e.time
    end)
    if _G.PianoState.ProgressConn then _G.PianoState.ProgressConn:Disconnect() end
    _G.PianoState.ProgressConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if _G.PianoState.Status == "Playing" then
            _G.PianoState.CurrentTime = _G.PianoState.CurrentTime + dt * _G.PianoState.Speed
        end
    end)
end

local function pianoStopPlayback()
    if _G.PianoState.PlaybackConn then
        _G.PianoState.PlaybackConn:Disconnect()
        _G.PianoState.PlaybackConn = nil
    end
    if _G.PianoState.ProgressConn then
        _G.PianoState.ProgressConn:Disconnect()
        _G.PianoState.ProgressConn = nil
    end
    _G.PianoState.Status = "Stopped"
    _G.PianoState.Index = 1
    _G.PianoState.LastTime = 0
    _G.PianoState.CurrentTime = 0
end

local function pianoSeek(time)
    local song = _G.PianoState.Song
    if #song == 0 then return end
    local idx = 1
    for i, e in ipairs(song) do
        if e.time >= time then
            idx = i
            break
        end
    end
    _G.PianoState.Index = idx
    _G.PianoState.LastTime = song[idx] and song[idx].time or 0
    _G.PianoState.CurrentTime = time
end

local function getHRP(char)
    if not char then return nil end
    return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
end

local function getNearestEnemy(maxRange)
    local myHRP = getHRP(lp.Character)
    if not myHRP then return nil end
    local best, bestDist = nil, maxRange
    local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
    local list = chars and chars:GetChildren() or {}
    if #list == 0 then
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= lp and p.Character then
                table.insert(list, p.Character)
            end
        end
    end
    for _, model in ipairs(list) do
        if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= lp.Character then
            local hrp = getHRP(model)
            local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < bestDist then
                    bestDist = dist
                    best = model
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

local function isEnemyRagdolled(targetChar)
    local hum = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = getHRP(targetChar)
    if hum and hrp then
        return hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or math.abs(hrp.CFrame.UpVector.Y) < 0.7
    end
    return false
end

local function isSelfStunnedOrRagdolled()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = getHRP(char)
    if not hum or not hrp then return false end
    if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then return true end
    if math.abs(hrp.CFrame.UpVector.Y) < 0.7 then return true end
    if char:GetAttribute("Stun") then return true end
    if char:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") then return true end
    return false
end
task.spawn(function()
    local ok, MovementController = pcall(function()
        return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Controllers.Character.MovementController)
    end)
    if not ok or not MovementController then return end
    local origDash = MovementController.Dash
    MovementController.Dash = function(self, player, char, direction)
        if player == lp then
        end
        return origDash(self, player, char, direction)
    end
end)

local BlockActivated = nil
local BlockDeactivated = nil

task.spawn(function()
    pcall(function()
        local knit = RS:WaitForChild("Knit", 10)
        if not knit then return end
        local knitF = knit:WaitForChild("Knit", 10)
        local services = knitF:WaitForChild("Services", 10)
        local dfs = services:WaitForChild("DivergentFistService", 10)
        if dfs then
            local re = dfs:WaitForChild("RE", 10)
        end
        local bs = services:WaitForChild("BlockService", 10)
        if bs then
            local re = bs:WaitForChild("RE", 10)
            BlockActivated = re:WaitForChild("Activated", 10)
            BlockDeactivated = re:WaitForChild("Deactivated", 10)
        end
    end)
end)

local ResetRemote = nil
task.spawn(function()
    local services = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Network"):WaitForChild("Services", 10)
    if services then
        ResetRemote = services:WaitForChild("ResetService", 10)
        if ResetRemote then
            ResetRemote = ResetRemote:WaitForChild("RE", 10)
            if ResetRemote then
                ResetRemote = ResetRemote:WaitForChild("ResetCharacter", 10)
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function getESPTargets()
    local targets = {}
    local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
    if chars then
        for _, char in ipairs(chars:GetChildren()) do
            if char:IsA("Model") and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and char ~= lp.Character then
                local p = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
                table.insert(targets, {
                    Model = char,
                    Player = p,
                    Name = p and (p.DisplayName or p.Name) or char.Name
                })
            end
        end
    end
    return targets
end

local function cleanESPObjects()
    for model, obj in pairs(_G.ESPState.Objects) do
        if obj.Highlight then pcall(function() obj.Highlight:Destroy() end) end
        if obj.Billboard then pcall(function() obj.Billboard:Destroy() end) end
    end
    for item, obj in pairs(_G.ESPState.ItemObjects) do
        if obj.Highlight then pcall(function() obj.Highlight:Destroy() end) end
        if obj.Billboard then pcall(function() obj.Billboard:Destroy() end) end
    end
    _G.ESPState.Objects = {}
    _G.ESPState.ItemObjects = {}
end

local function stopESP()
    if _G.ESPState.UpdateLoop then
        _G.ESPState.UpdateLoop:Disconnect()
        _G.ESPState.UpdateLoop = nil
    end
    cleanESPObjects()
end

local function startESP()
    if _G.ESPState.UpdateLoop then
        _G.ESPState.UpdateLoop:Disconnect()
        _G.ESPState.UpdateLoop = nil
    end
    cleanESPObjects()
    _G.ESPState.UpdateLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local chams = _G.ESPState.ChamsEnabled
        local names = _G.ESPState.NameEnabled
        local chars = _G.ESPState.CharacterEnabled
        local items = _G.ESPState.ItemsEnabled
        if not (chams or names or chars or items) then
            stopESP()
            return
        end
        for model, obj in pairs(_G.ESPState.Objects) do
            if not model or not model.Parent then
                if obj.Highlight then pcall(function() obj.Highlight:Destroy() end) end
                if obj.Billboard then pcall(function() obj.Billboard:Destroy() end) end
                _G.ESPState.Objects[model] = nil
            end
        end
        for _, target in ipairs(getESPTargets()) do
            local model = target.Model
            local pRoot = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model:FindFirstChild("Torso") or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            if not _G.ESPState.Objects[model] then
                _G.ESPState.Objects[model] = {}
            end
            local obj = _G.ESPState.Objects[model]
            if chams then
                if not obj.Highlight or obj.Highlight.Parent ~= model then
                    if obj.Highlight then pcall(function() obj.Highlight:Destroy() end) end
                    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                    highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                    highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
                    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                    highlight.Parent = model
                    obj.Highlight = highlight
                end
                obj.Highlight.Enabled = true
            else
                if obj.Highlight then obj.Highlight.Enabled = false end
            end
            if (names or chars) and pRoot then
                if not obj.Billboard or obj.Billboard.Parent ~= pRoot then
                    if obj.Billboard then pcall(function() obj.Billboard:Destroy() end) end
                    local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
                    bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
                    bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
                    local list = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                    list.Parent = bb
                    list.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
                    list.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
                    local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    nameLabel.Name = "NameLabel"
                    nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 15)
                    nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                    nameLabel.TextSize = 14
                    nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    nameLabel.Parent = bb
                    local charLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    charLabel.Name = "CharLabel"
                    charLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 15)
                    charLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    charLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 200, 255)
                    charLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                    charLabel.TextSize = 12
                    charLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                    charLabel.Parent = bb
                    bb.Parent = pRoot
                    obj.Billboard = bb
                end
                obj.Billboard.Enabled = true
                local nameLabel = obj.Billboard:FindFirstChild("NameLabel")
                local charLabel = obj.Billboard:FindFirstChild("CharLabel")
                if nameLabel then
                    nameLabel.Visible = names
                    nameLabel.Text = target.Name
                end
                if charLabel then
                    charLabel.Visible = chars
                    charLabel.Text = "[" .. model.Name .. "]"
                end
            else
                if obj.Billboard then obj.Billboard.Enabled = false end
            end
        end
        local itemsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Items")
        if itemsFolder then
            if items then
                for _, item in ipairs(itemsFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    if not _G.ESPState.ItemObjects[item] then
                        _G.ESPState.ItemObjects[item] = {}
                        local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                        highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
                        highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                        highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
                        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                        highlight.Parent = item
                        _G.ESPState.ItemObjects[item].Highlight = highlight
                        local primaryPart = item:IsA("BasePart") and item or item:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                        if primaryPart then
                            local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                            bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
                            bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
                            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
                            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                            label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                            label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)
                            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                            label.TextScaled = true
                            label.Text = item.Name
                            label.Parent = bb
                            bb.Parent = primaryPart
                            _G.ESPState.ItemObjects[item].Billboard = bb
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                for item, obj in pairs(_G.ESPState.ItemObjects) do
                    if obj.Highlight then pcall(function() obj.Highlight:Destroy() end) end
                    if obj.Billboard then pcall(function() obj.Billboard:Destroy() end) end
                    _G.ESPState.ItemObjects[item] = nil
                end
            end
        end
        for item, obj in pairs(_G.ESPState.ItemObjects) do
            if not item or not item.Parent then
                if obj.Highlight then pcall(function() obj.Highlight:Destroy() end) end
                if obj.Billboard then pcall(function() obj.Billboard:Destroy() end) end
                _G.ESPState.ItemObjects[item] = nil
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function findBlockBreakTarget()
    local myChar = lp.Character
    local myHRP = getHRP(myChar)
    if not myChar or not myHRP then return nil end
    local detectionDist = _G.BlockBreakState.Distance
    local best = nil
    local bestDist = detectionDist
    local chars = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
    local list = chars and chars:GetChildren() or {}
    if #list == 0 then
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= lp and p.Character then
                table.insert(list, p.Character)
            end
        end
    end
    for _, model in ipairs(list) do
        if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= myChar then
            local hrp = getHRP(model)
            local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                if dist <= bestDist then
                    bestDist = dist
                    best = model
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

local function fireBlockBreakDash(target)
    if _G.BlockBreakState.IsDashing then return end
    local myChar = lp.Character
    local myHRP = getHRP(myChar)
    local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local targetHRP = getHRP(target)
    if not myChar or not myHRP or not myHum or not targetHRP then return end
    _G.BlockBreakState.IsDashing = true
    if _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn then
        _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn:Disconnect()
        _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn = nil
    end
    local startTime = tick()
    local startPos = Vector3.new(myHRP.Position.X, 0, myHRP.Position.Z)
    local right = Vector3.new(targetHRP.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, targetHRP.CFrame.RightVector.Z).Unit
    local toUs = Vector3.new(myHRP.Position.X, 0, myHRP.Position.Z) - Vector3.new(targetHRP.Position.X, 0, targetHRP.Position.Z)
    local curveOffset = targetHRP.Position + right * _G.BlockBreakState.CurveStrength * (toUs:Dot(right) >= 0 and 1 or -1)
    local curvePos = Vector3.new(curveOffset.X, 0, curveOffset.Z)
    local targetBehindOffset = targetHRP.Position - targetHRP.CFrame.LookVector * _G.BlockBreakState.Radius
    local endPos = Vector3.new(targetBehindOffset.X, 0, targetBehindOffset.Z)
    myHum.AutoRotate = false
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://3929467229"
    sound.Parent = myHRP
    sound:Play()
    game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound, 2)
    local trailPart
    pcall(function()
        local smokeTrail = RS:WaitForChild("Utils"):WaitForChild("Misc"):WaitForChild("SmokeTrail")
        trailPart = smokeTrail:Clone()
        trailPart.CFrame = myHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -2.5, 0)
        local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
        weld.Part0 = myHRP
        weld.Part1 = trailPart
        weld.Parent = trailPart
        trailPart.Parent = myChar
    end)
    _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local t = math.clamp((tick() - startTime) / _G.BlockBreakState.Duration, 0, 1)
        local e = easeOut(t)
        if t >= 1 or not targetHRP.Parent then
            _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn:Disconnect()
            _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn = nil
            _G.BlockBreakState.IsDashing = false
            if trailPart then trailPart:Destroy() end
            if _G.BlockBreakState.LockBehind then
                local endLockTime = tick() + 0.35
                local lockConn
                lockConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if tick() >= endLockTime or not targetHRP.Parent or myHum.Health <= 0 then
                        lockConn:Disconnect()
                        myHum.AutoRotate = true
                    else
                        myHRP.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(myHRP.Position, Vector3.new(targetHRP.Position.X, myHRP.Position.Y, targetHRP.Position.Z))
                    end
                end)
                table.insert(_G.BlockBreakState.Connections, lockConn)
            else
                myHum.AutoRotate = true
            end
            return
        end
        local midPos = curvePos:Lerp(endPos, e)
        local currentPos = startPos:Lerp(midPos, e)
        local finalPos = Vector3.new(currentPos.X, myHRP.Position.Y, currentPos.Z)
        if _G.BlockBreakState.LockBehind then
            myHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(finalPos, Vector3.new(targetHRP.Position.X, finalPos.Y, targetHRP.Position.Z))
        else
            myHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(finalPos) * (myHRP.CFrame - myHRP.CFrame.Position)
        end
    end)
end

local function hookBlockBreak(char)
    if not char then return end
    if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) == lp then return end
    local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if hum then
        local animator = hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
        if animator then
                        table.insert(_G.BlockBreakState.Connections, animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                if not _G.BlockBreakState.Enabled or _G.BlockBreakState.IsDashing then return end
                local blockAnimList = {
                    ["rbxassetid://10468665991"] = true,
                    ["rbxassetid://104686659910"] = true,
                }
                if blockAnimList[track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId or ""] then
                    local target = findBlockBreakTarget()
                    if target then
                        executeBlockBreakDash(target)
                    end
                end
            end))
        end
    end
end

local function disconnectBlockBreak()
    for _, conn in ipairs(_G.BlockBreakState.Connections) do
        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
    end
    _G.BlockBreakState.Connections = {}
    if _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn then
        _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn:Disconnect()
        _G.BlockBreakState.DashConn = nil
    end
    _G.BlockBreakState.IsDashing = false
end

local function startBlockBreak()
    disconnectBlockBreak()
    local charFolder = workspace:WaitForChild("Characters", 10)
    if charFolder then
        for _, child in ipairs(charFolder:GetChildren()) do
            hookBlockBreak(child)
        end
        table.insert(_G.BlockBreakState.Connections, charFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            task.wait(0.5)
            hookBlockBreak(child)
        end))
    end
end

local function autoBlockTrigger()
    if _G.AutoBlockState.GlobalBlockCount == 0 then
        if BlockActivated then
            pcall(function() BlockActivated:FireServer() end)
        end
    end
    _G.AutoBlockState.GlobalBlockCount = _G.AutoBlockState.GlobalBlockCount + 1
end

local function autoBlockRelease()
    _G.AutoBlockState.GlobalBlockCount = math.max(0, _G.AutoBlockState.GlobalBlockCount - 1)
    if _G.AutoBlockState.GlobalBlockCount == 0 then
        local ok = pcall(function()
            if BlockDeactivated then
                BlockDeactivated:FireServer()
            end
        end)
        if not ok then
            _G.AutoBlockState.GlobalBlockCount = 0
        end
    end
end

local function triggerBlockCooldown()
    if not _G.AutoBlockState.Enabled or _G.AutoBlockState.IsOnCooldown then return end
    if not BlockActivated or not BlockDeactivated then return end
    _G.AutoBlockState.IsOnCooldown = true
    task.spawn(function()
        autoBlockTrigger()
        task.wait(_G.ABSettings.BlockDuration)
        autoBlockRelease()
        task.wait(_G.ABSettings.CooldownTime)
        _G.AutoBlockState.IsOnCooldown = false
    end)
end

local function monitorTrail(child)
    if child and child.Name == "CombatTrail" and child:IsA("BasePart") then
        table.insert(combatTrails, child)
        local conn
        conn = child.Destroying:Connect(function()
            for idx, trail in ipairs(combatTrails) do
                if trail == child then
                    table.remove(combatTrails, idx)
                    break
                end
            end
            conn:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
end

local function isPlayerAttacking()
    local char = lp.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        if track.IsPlaying and track.Animation then
            local animName = string.lower(track.Animation.Name or "")
            local isMovement = false
            for _, moveName in ipairs(movementAnims) do
                if string.find(animName, moveName) then
                    isMovement = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not isMovement then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function isDeadOrRagdolled()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return true end
    if char:GetAttribute("Dead") then return true end
    local ragdoll = char:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")
    if ragdoll and ragdoll > 0 then return true end
    return false
end

local function isFeinting(character)
    local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return false end
    if hrp:FindFirstChild("Feint") then return true end
    return false
end

local function isFacing(attackerHRP, targetHRP, threshold)
    local attackerLook = attackerHRP.CFrame.LookVector
    local toTarget = (targetHRP.Position - attackerHRP.Position).Unit
    return attackerLook:Dot(toTarget) >= threshold
end

local function handleProjectileAnim(attacker, animTrack)
    if not _G.ABSettings.ProjectileBlockEnabled then return end
    if not animTrack or not animTrack.Animation then return end
    local animId = string.match(tostring(animTrack.Animation.AnimationId), "%d+")
    if not animId or not autoBlockAnims[animId] then return end
    if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(attacker) == lp then return end
    if isDeadOrRagdolled() then return end
    local myChar = lp.Character
    local myHRP = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local attackerHRP = attacker:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myHRP or not attackerHRP then return end
    local distance = (attackerHRP.Position - myHRP.Position).Magnitude
    if distance > _G.ABSettings.ProjectileRange then return end
    if _G.ABSettings.ProjectileFacingCheck and not isFacing(attackerHRP, myHRP, _G.ABSettings.ProjectileFacingThreshold) then return end
    if _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] then return end
    _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] = true
    autoBlockTrigger()
    local stoppedConn
    stoppedConn = animTrack.Stopped:Once(function()
        if _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] then
            _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] = nil
            autoBlockRelease()
        end
    end)
    table.insert(_G.AutoBlockState.Connections, stoppedConn)
    task.delay(5, function()
        if _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] then
            _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] = nil
            autoBlockRelease()
        end
    end)
end

local function handleMoveAnim(attacker, animTrack)
    if not _G.ABSettings.MoveBlockEnabled then return end
    if not animTrack or not animTrack.Animation then return end
    local animId = string.match(tostring(animTrack.Animation.AnimationId), "%d+")
    if animId and autoBlockAnims[animId] then return end
    local animName = string.lower(animTrack.Animation.Name or "")
    for _, moveName in ipairs(movementAnims) do
        if string.find(animName, moveName) then return end
    end
    if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(attacker) == lp then return end
    if not _G.ABSettings.BlockWhileAttacking and isPlayerAttacking() then return end
    if isDeadOrRagdolled() then return end
    if attacker:GetAttribute("Dead") then return end
    local ragdoll = attacker:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")
    if ragdoll and ragdoll > 0 then return end
    local myChar = lp.Character
    local myHRP = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local attackerHRP = attacker:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myHRP or not attackerHRP then return end
    local distance = (attackerHRP.Position - myHRP.Position).Magnitude
    if distance > _G.ABSettings.MoveDetectionRange then return end
    local look = attackerHRP.CFrame.LookVector
    local toUs = (myHRP.Position - attackerHRP.Position).Unit
    if look:Dot(toUs) < _G.ABSettings.FacingAngleThreshold then return end
    if isFeinting(attacker) then return end
    if _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] then return end
    _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] = true
    autoBlockTrigger()
    local stoppedConn
    stoppedConn = animTrack.Stopped:Once(function()
        if _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] then
            _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] = nil
            autoBlockRelease()
        end
    end)
    table.insert(_G.AutoBlockState.Connections, stoppedConn)
    task.delay(5, function()
        if _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] then
            _G.AutoBlockState.ActiveBlockingTracks[animTrack] = nil
            autoBlockRelease()
        end
    end)
end

local function monitorCharacter(char)
    if not char then return end
    if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) == lp then return end
    local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if hum then
        local animator = hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
        if animator then
                        table.insert(_G.AutoBlockState.Connections, animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                handleMoveAnim(char, track)
                handleProjectileAnim(char, track)
            end))
        end
    end
end

local function disconnectAutoBlock()
    for _, conn in ipairs(_G.AutoBlockState.Connections) do
        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
    end
    _G.AutoBlockState.Connections = {}
    combatTrails = {}
end

local function startAutoBlock()
    disconnectAutoBlock()
    local EffectsFolder = workspace:WaitForChild("Effects", 10)
    if EffectsFolder then
        for _, child in ipairs(EffectsFolder:GetChildren()) do
            monitorTrail(child)
        end
        table.insert(_G.AutoBlockState.Connections, EffectsFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(monitorTrail))
    end
    table.insert(_G.AutoBlockState.Connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.AutoBlockState.Enabled or _G.AutoBlockState.IsOnCooldown then return end
        local myChar = lp.Character
        local myHRP = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myHRP then return end
        for _, trail in ipairs(combatTrails) do
            if trail.Parent then
                local toTrail = trail.Position - myHRP.Position
                local distance = toTrail.Magnitude
                if distance <= _G.ABSettings.MaxDistance and distance > _G.ABSettings.MinDistance then
                    if myHRP.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(toTrail.Unit) >= _G.ABSettings.AheadThreshold then
                        triggerBlockCooldown()
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end))
    local charFolder = workspace:WaitForChild("Characters", 10)
    if charFolder then
        for _, child in ipairs(charFolder:GetChildren()) do
            monitorCharacter(child)
        end
        table.insert(_G.AutoBlockState.Connections, charFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            task.wait(0.5)
            monitorCharacter(child)
        end))
    end
end

if not _G.CharState.HitboxHooked then
    _G.CharState.HitboxHooked = true
    pcall(function()
        local Knit = require(RS:WaitForChild("Knit", 10):WaitForChild("Knit", 10))
        local HitboxController = Knit.GetController("HitboxController")
        local originalSphereHitbox = HitboxController.SphereHitbox
        HitboxController.SphereHitbox = function(self, arg2, arg3, size, ...)
            local expandedSize = size * (_G.CharState.HitboxExpander and _G.CharState.HitboxSize or 1)
            local success, result = pcall(originalSphereHitbox, self, arg2, arg3, expandedSize, ...)
            if success then return result end
            return originalSphereHitbox(self, arg2, arg3, size, ...)
        end
    end)
end

local function hookCharacter(char)
    if not char then return end
    local ragdollSignal = char:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Ragdoll")
    table.insert(_G.CharState.Connections, ragdollSignal:Connect(function()
        if _G.CharState.AntiRagdoll and char:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") == 1 then
            char:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", 0)
        end
    end))
    local info = char:WaitForChild("Info", 5)
    if info then
        table.insert(_G.CharState.Connections, info.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if _G.CharState.AntiStun then
                local isStun = (child.Name == "Stun")
                local isKnockback = (child.Name == "Knockback")
                local target = _G.CharState.AntiStunTarget
                if (target == "All" and (isStun or isKnockback))
                   or (target == "Stun Only" and isStun)
                   or (target == "Knockback Only" and isKnockback) then
                    task.defer(function() child:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end))
    end
    local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    if hum then
        local deathConn = hum.Died:Connect(function()
            if _G.MiscState.IsInvisible then
                local elem = UIElements and UIElements["Invisible"]
                if elem and elem.Set then
                    pcall(function() elem:Set(false) end)
                end
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.CharState.Connections, deathConn)
        local animator = hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
        if animator then
            local animList = {
                ["FallAnim"] = true, ["Animation1"] = true, ["Animation2"] = true,
                ["WalkAnim"] = true, ["JumpAnim"] = true, ["RunAnim"] = true,
                ["idle"] = true, ["walk"] = true, ["walkL"] = true, ["walkR"] = true,
                ["sprint"] = true, ["jump"] = true, ["fall"] = true, ["climb"] = true,
                ["sit"] = true, ["Land"] = true, ["Roll"] = true
            }
            table.insert(_G.CharState.Connections, animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                if _G.CharState.SilentAnim then
                    if animList[track.Name] then
                        track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2
                    elseif track.Name == "ToolNoneAnim" and (track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId ~= "rbxassetid://123456789") then
                        track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
                    end
                end

                local animId = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId or ""
                if _G.SpecialsState.AutoDoorHakari and animId == "rbxassetid://82541714192027" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.2)
                        pcall(function()
                            local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                            local service = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("ShutterDoorService")
                            if service then
                                local moveset = char:WaitForChild("Moveset", 5)
                                local item = moveset and moveset:WaitForChild("Shutter Doors", 5)
                                if item then service.RE.Activated:FireServer(item) end
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if _G.SpecialsState.FeverCrusher and animId == "rbxassetid://108123475959041" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.2)
                        pcall(function()
                            local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                            local service = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("ShutterDoorService")
                            if service then
                                local moveset = char:WaitForChild("Moveset", 5)
                                local item = moveset and moveset:WaitForChild("Shutter Doors", 5)
                                if item then service.RE.Activated:FireServer(item) end
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if _G.SpecialsState.AutoBodyRepel and animId == "rbxassetid://108319980293313" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.2)
                        pcall(function()
                            local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                            local service = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("FocusStrikeService")
                            if service then
                                local moveset = char:WaitForChild("Moveset", 5)
                                local item = moveset and moveset:WaitForChild("Focus Strike", 5)
                                if item then service.RE.Activated:FireServer(item) end
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if _G.SpecialsState.AutoWCS and animId == "rbxassetid://131506102901134" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        pcall(function()
                            local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                            local rushService = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("RushService")
                            local flameService = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("FlameArrowService")
                            local itadoriService = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("ItadoriService")
                            local moveset = char:WaitForChild("Moveset", 5)
                            if rushService then rushService.RE.Activated:FireServer(moveset:WaitForChild("Rush", 5)) end
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            if flameService then flameService.RE.Activated:FireServer(moveset:WaitForChild("Open", 5)) end
                            task.wait(1.1)
                            if itadoriService then
                                for i = 1, 8 do
                                    itadoriService.RE.RightActivated:FireServer()
                                    task.wait(0.05)
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if _G.SpecialsState.HeianSukunaBF and string.find(animId, "110906451704074") then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        if _G.SpecialsState.CleaveDelay > 0 then task.wait(_G.SpecialsState.CleaveDelay) end
                        pcall(function()
                            local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                            local service = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("CleaveService") or Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("CleaveRushService")
                            local moveset = char:WaitForChild("Moveset", 5)
                            local item = moveset and moveset:WaitForChild("Cleave Rush", 5)
                            if service and item then service.RE.Activated:FireServer(item) end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if _G.SpecialsState.MahitoBF and string.find(animId, "72475960800126") then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.2)
                        pcall(function()
                            local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                            local service = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("FocusStrikeService")
                            local moveset = char:WaitForChild("Moveset", 5)
                            local item = moveset and moveset:WaitForChild("Focus Strike", 5)
                            if service and item then service.RE.Activated:FireServer(item) end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if _G.SpecialsState.PerfectSwap and SWAP_ANIMATIONS[animId] then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.45)
                        pcall(function()
                            local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                            local todoService = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("TodoService")
                            if todoService then todoService.RE.Activated:FireServer(false) end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
                if _G.SpecialsState.YutaBF and string.find(animId, "89582140026963") then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.3)
                        pcall(function()
                            local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                            local service = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild("ResoluteSlashService")
                            local moveset = char:WaitForChild("Moveset", 5)
                            local item = moveset and moveset:WaitForChild("Resolute Slash", 5)
                            if service and item then service.RE.Activated:FireServer(item) end
                        end)
                    end)
                end
            end))
        end
    end
end

if lp.Character then hookCharacter(lp.Character) end
table.insert(_G.CharState.Connections, lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookCharacter))

local function getRandomPlayer()
    local targetPool = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= lp and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            table.insert(targetPool, p)
        end
    end
    if #targetPool == 0 then return nil end
    return targetPool[math.random(1, #targetPool)]
end

local function getLocalRootCFrame()
    local char = lp.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return root and root.CFrame or nil
end

local function safeTeleportTo(targetPlayer)
    local localChar = lp.Character
    local targetChar = targetPlayer.Character
    if not localChar or not targetChar then return false end
    local localRoot = localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if localRoot and targetRoot then
        localRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4)
        return true
    end
    return false
end

local function checkRubberBand(originalCFrame)
    local currentCFrame = getLocalRootCFrame()
    if not currentCFrame or not originalCFrame then return true end
    return (currentCFrame.Position - originalCFrame.Position).Magnitude < 8
end

local function triggerNetworkReset()
    pcall(function()
        if ResetRemote then ResetRemote:FireServer() end
    end)
end

local function waitForCharacterRespawn(oldCharacter)
    local startTime = tick()
    local timeout = 15
    local playerName = lp.Name
    local charactersFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters") or workspace:WaitForChild("Characters", 5)
    while tick() - startTime < timeout do
        local parentToSearch = charactersFolder or workspace
        local potentialChar = parentToSearch:FindFirstChild(playerName)
        if potentialChar and potentialChar ~= oldCharacter and potentialChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            if lp.Character == potentialChar then break end
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    task.wait(0.5)
end

local function executeACBypass()
    if _G.MiscState.ACBypassActive then return end
    _G.MiscState.ACBypassActive = true
    local attempts = 0
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.MiscState.ACBypassEnabled and _G.MiscState.ACBypassActive do
            local startingCFrame = getLocalRootCFrame()
            if not startingCFrame then
                task.wait(1)
            else
                local target = getRandomPlayer()
                if not target then
                    create_notification({ name = "No valid player found", duration = 2 })
                    task.wait(2)
                else
                    attempts = attempts + 1
                    create_notification({ name = "Attempt " .. attempts .. " -> " .. target.Name, duration = 1 })
                    if safeTeleportTo(target) then
                        task.wait(0.35)
                        if checkRubberBand(startingCFrame) then
                            create_notification({ name = "Rubber-banded - resetting...", duration = 1 })
                            local oldChar = lp.Character
                            triggerNetworkReset()
                            waitForCharacterRespawn(oldChar)
                        else
                            create_notification({ name = "Verifying bypass...", duration = 1 })
                            local verifyTarget = getRandomPlayer() or target
                            local preVerifyCFrame = getLocalRootCFrame()
                            if safeTeleportTo(verifyTarget) then
                                task.wait(0.4)
                                if checkRubberBand(startingCFrame) or checkRubberBand(preVerifyCFrame) then
                                    create_notification({ name = "Fluke detected - resetting...", duration = 1 })
                                    local oldChar = lp.Character
                                    triggerNetworkReset()
                                    waitForCharacterRespawn(oldChar)
                                else
                                    create_notification({ name = "AC Bypass Successful!", duration = 3 })
                                    task.wait(0.5)
                                    local cleanChar = lp.Character
                                    if cleanChar and cleanChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                        cleanChar.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = startingCFrame
                                    end
                                    _G.MiscState.ACBypassEnabled = false
                                    _G.MiscState.ACBypassActive = false
                                    local elem = UIElements and UIElements["AC Bypass"]
                                    if elem and elem.Set then elem:Set(false) end
                                    break
                                end
                            else
                                task.wait(0.5)
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        _G.MiscState.ACBypassActive = false
    end)
end

local function executeRapidTP()
    if _G.MiscState.RapidTPActive then return end
    _G.MiscState.RapidTPActive = true
    _G.MiscState.RapidTPSavedCFrame = getLocalRootCFrame()
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.MiscState.RapidTPEnabled and _G.MiscState.RapidTPActive do
            local playersList = Players:GetPlayers()
            for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(playersList) do
                if not _G.MiscState.RapidTPEnabled or not _G.MiscState.RapidTPActive then break end
                if targetPlayer ~= lp then
                    local localChar = lp.Character
                    local targetChar = targetPlayer.Character
                    if localChar and targetChar then
                        local localRoot = localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if localRoot and targetRoot then
                            localRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        _G.MiscState.RapidTPActive = false
        if _G.MiscState.RapidTPSavedCFrame then
            task.wait(0.15)
            local char = lp.Character
            if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = _G.MiscState.RapidTPSavedCFrame
            end
            _G.MiscState.RapidTPSavedCFrame = nil
        end
    end)
end

local function AutoM1()
    local virtualinpt = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
    virtualinpt:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 0)
    task.wait(0.01)
    virtualinpt:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 0)
end

local function WatchSwap()
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
    local hrp = char.HumanoidRootPart
    local timeElapsed = 0
    local lastPos = hrp.Position
    local lastLook = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
    local connection
    connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        timeElapsed = timeElapsed + dt
        local currentPos = hrp.Position
        local currentLook = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
        local distanceMoved = (currentPos - lastPos).Magnitude
        local rotationChange = (currentLook - lastLook).Magnitude
        if distanceMoved > 1.5 or rotationChange > 0.5 then
            connection:Disconnect()
            task.wait(0.04)
            AutoM1()
            return
        end
        if timeElapsed >= 0.5 then
            connection:Disconnect()
            task.wait(0.01)
            AutoM1()
            return
        end
        lastPos = currentPos
        lastLook = currentLook
    end)
end

UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    
    if input.KeyCode == _G.MiscState.Flight.Keybind then
        local flying = not _G.MiscState.Flight.IsFlying
        if _G.FlightToggleCallback then _G.FlightToggleCallback(flying) end
    end
    if _G.SpecialsState.AutoM1Enabled and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.R then
        WatchSwap()
    end
end)

RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not _G.LockState.Enabled then
        _G.LockState.CurrentLockTarget = nil
        return
    end
    if _G.LockSettings.Sticky and _G.LockState.CurrentLockTarget then
        local target = _G.LockState.CurrentLockTarget
        local hum = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not target.Parent or (hum and hum.Health <= 0) then
            _G.LockState.CurrentLockTarget = nil
        end
    end
    if not _G.LockState.CurrentLockTarget then
        _G.LockState.CurrentLockTarget = getNearestEnemy(_G.BFSettings.Range)
    end
    local target = _G.LockState.CurrentLockTarget
    local myChar = lp.Character
    if not target or not myChar then return end
    local targetHRP = target:FindFirstChild(_G.LockSettings.TargetPart or "HumanoidRootPart") or getHRP(target)
    if not targetHRP then return end
    local method = _G.LockSettings.Method or "Camera"
    if method == "Camera" then
        local cf = Camera.CFrame
        Camera.CFrame = cf:Lerp(
            CFrame.lookAt(
                Camera.CFrame.Position,
                targetHRP.Position + Camera.CFrame.RightVector * (_G.LockSettings.SideOffset or 0)
            ),
            0.4
        )
    else
        local hum = myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local myHRP = getHRP(myChar)
        if hum and myHRP then
            hum.AutoRotate = false
            if not _G.LockState.LockBodyGyro or not _G.LockState.LockBodyGyro.Parent then
                if _G.LockState.LockBodyGyro then _G.LockState.LockBodyGyro:Destroy() end
                local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 400000, 0)
                bg.P = 50000
                bg.D = 500
                bg.Parent = myHRP
                _G.LockState.LockBodyGyro = bg
            end
            _G.LockState.LockBodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
                myHRP.Position,
                Vector3.new(targetHRP.Position.X, myHRP.Position.Y, targetHRP.Position.Z)
            )
        end
    end
end)

local function stopFlight()
    _G.MiscState.Flight.IsFlying = false
    if _G.MiscState.Flight.Loop then
        _G.MiscState.Flight.Loop:Disconnect()
        _G.MiscState.Flight.Loop = nil
    end
    if _G.MiscState.Flight.BV then
        _G.MiscState.Flight.BV:Destroy()
        _G.MiscState.Flight.BV = nil
    end
    if _G.MiscState.Flight.BG then
        _G.MiscState.Flight.BG:Destroy()
        _G.MiscState.Flight.BG = nil
    end
    local char = lp.Character
    if char then
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.PlatformStand = false end
    end
    Camera.FieldOfView = 70
end

local function startFlight()
    _G.MiscState.Flight.IsFlying = true
    local char = lp.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return end
    hum.PlatformStand = true
    local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
    bv.Parent = hrp
    _G.MiscState.Flight.BV = bv
    local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    bg.P = 9e4
    bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
    bg.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
    bg.Parent = hrp
    _G.MiscState.Flight.BG = bg
    _G.MiscState.Flight.Loop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not _G.MiscState.Flight.IsFlying then return end
        local char2 = lp.Character
        if not char2 then return end
        local hrp2 = char2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum2 = char2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp2 or not hum2 or hum2.Health <= 0 then
            stopFlight()
            return
        end
        local dirX, dirY, dirZ = 0, 0, 0
        local speed = _G.MiscState.Flight.BaseSpeed or 150
        local cf = Camera.CFrame
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dirX = dirX + cf.LookVector.X; dirY = dirY + cf.LookVector.Y; dirZ = dirZ + cf.LookVector.Z end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dirX = dirX - cf.LookVector.X; dirY = dirY - cf.LookVector.Y; dirZ = dirZ - cf.LookVector.Z end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dirX = dirX - cf.RightVector.X; dirY = dirY - cf.RightVector.Y; dirZ = dirZ - cf.RightVector.Z end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dirX = dirX + cf.RightVector.X; dirY = dirY + cf.RightVector.Y; dirZ = dirZ + cf.RightVector.Z end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then dirY = dirY + 1 end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) then dirY = dirY - 1 end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then speed = _G.MiscState.Flight.SprintSpeed or 300 end
        local dir = Vector3.new(dirX, dirY, dirZ)
        if dir.Magnitude > 0 then dir = dir.Unit end
        bv.Velocity = dir * speed
        bg.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp2.Position, hrp2.Position + cf.LookVector)
        local targetFOV = dir.Magnitude > 0 and (_G.MiscState.Flight.MaxFOV or 120) or 70
        Camera.FieldOfView = Camera.FieldOfView + (targetFOV - Camera.FieldOfView) * math.clamp(10 * dt, 0, 1)
    end)
end

_G.FlightToggleCallback = function(v)
    if v then startFlight() else stopFlight() end
end
