local r24 = {
    create_notification = function(self, tbl)
        if MacLib.Notification then
            MacLib:Notification({
                Title = tbl.name or "Sosy Hub",
                Content = tbl.content or "",
                Lifetime = tbl.duration or tbl.Lifetime or 3
            })
        else
            game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = tbl.name or "Sosy Hub",
                Text = tbl.content or "",
                Duration = tbl.duration or tbl.Lifetime or 3
            })
        end
    end
}

local function create_notification(tbl)
    r24:create_notification(tbl)
end

local function teleportTo(pos)
    if _G.TeleportState.IsTeleporting then return end
    _G.TeleportState.IsTeleporting = true
    local char = lp.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then
        _G.TeleportState.IsTeleporting = false
        return
    end
    local targetCF = typeof(pos) == "Vector3" and CFrame.new(pos) or pos
    local conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local myChar = lp.Character
        local myHRP = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local myHum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if myHRP and myHum then
            local oldCF = myHRP.CFrame
            myHum.CameraOffset = targetCF:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(oldCF.Position)).Position
            myHRP.CFrame = targetCF
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            myHRP.CFrame = oldCF
            myHum.CameraOffset = oldCF:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(oldCF.Position)).Position
        end
    end)
    task.wait(0.1)
    char:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, -457, 0))
    task.wait()
    if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
    hum.CameraOffset = Vector3.zero
    char:PivotTo(targetCF)
    hrp.CFrame = targetCF
    task.wait(0.1)
    _G.TeleportState.IsTeleporting = false
end

local function getLocationsList()
    local list = {}
    for name, _ in pairs(r121) do table.insert(list, name) end
    for name, _ in pairs(_G.TeleportState.UserSavedLocations or {}) do
        if not r121[name] then table.insert(list, name) end
    end
    table.sort(list)
    return list
end

local function getItemsList()
    local list = {}
    local items = workspace:FindFirstChild("Items")
    if items then
        for _, item in ipairs(items:GetChildren()) do
            table.insert(list, item.Name)
        end
    end
    table.sort(list)
    return list
end

local isGrabbing = false
local function grabItem()
    if isGrabbing then return end
    if _G.GrabberState.SelectedGrabItem == "" then
        create_notification({ name = "Select an item first!", duration = 2 })
        return
    end
    local items = workspace:FindFirstChild("Items")
    local item = items and items:FindFirstChild(_G.GrabberState.SelectedGrabItem)
    if not item then
        create_notification({ name = "Item no longer exists!", duration = 2 })
        return
    end
    local prompt = item:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
    if not prompt then
        create_notification({ name = "Item has no ProximityPrompt!", duration = 2 })
        return
    end
    local part = prompt.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and prompt.Parent or item:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    if not part then return end
    local char = lp.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return end
    isGrabbing = true
    create_notification({ name = "Flicker-Grabbing Item...", duration = 2 })
    task.spawn(function()
        local targetCF = part.CFrame
        local endTime = tick() + 1.2
        local oldCF = hrp.CFrame
        while tick() < endTime do
            hrp.CFrame = targetCF
            hum.CameraOffset = targetCF:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(oldCF.Position)).Position
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            if fireproximityprompt then fireproximityprompt(prompt) end
            hrp.CFrame = oldCF
            hum.CameraOffset = oldCF:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(oldCF.Position)).Position
            task.wait()
        end
        create_notification({ name = "Grab sequence complete!", duration = 2 })
        task.delay(2, function() isGrabbing = false end)
    end)
end

local function getConfigs()
    local configs = {}
    if isfolder and isfolder("SosyHUB") then
        pcall(function()
            for _, file in ipairs(listfiles("SosyHUB")) do
                local name = file:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
                if name then table.insert(configs, name) end
            end
        end)
    end
    table.sort(configs)
    return configs
end

local function saveConfig(name)
    if name == "" then
        create_notification({ name = "Please enter a name!", duration = 2 })
        return
    end
    if not isfolder("SosyHUB") then makefolder("SosyHUB") end
    local configData = {
        BFSettings = _G.BFSettings,
        ABSettings = _G.ABSettings,
        LockSettings = _G.LockSettings,
        LockState = { Enabled = _G.LockState.Enabled },
        BlockBreakState = {
            Enabled = _G.BlockBreakState.Enabled,
            LockBehind = _G.BlockBreakState.LockBehind,
            Distance = _G.BlockBreakState.Distance,
            Radius = _G.BlockBreakState.Radius,
            Duration = _G.BlockBreakState.Duration,
            CurveStrength = _G.BlockBreakState.CurveStrength
        },
        AutoBlockState = { Enabled = _G.AutoBlockState.Enabled },
        ESPState = {
            ChamsEnabled = _G.ESPState.ChamsEnabled,
            NameEnabled = _G.ESPState.NameEnabled,
            CharacterEnabled = _G.ESPState.CharacterEnabled,
            ItemsEnabled = _G.ESPState.ItemsEnabled
        },
        SpecialsState = {
            NanamiRatio = _G.SpecialsState.NanamiRatio,
            GarudaRebound = _G.SpecialsState.GarudaRebound,
            GarudaDelay = _G.SpecialsState.GarudaDelay,
            AutoWCS = _G.SpecialsState.AutoWCS,
            NaoyaDashActive = _G.SpecialsState.NaoyaDashActive,
            HiguQTE = _G.SpecialsState.HiguQTE,
            AutoDomain = _G.SpecialsState.AutoDomain,
            AutoDoorHakari = _G.SpecialsState.AutoDoorHakari,
            FeverCrusher = _G.SpecialsState.FeverCrusher,
            AutoBodyRepel = _G.SpecialsState.AutoBodyRepel,
            HeianSukunaBF = _G.SpecialsState.HeianSukunaBF,
            CleaveDelay = _G.SpecialsState.CleaveDelay,
            MahitoBF = _G.SpecialsState.MahitoBF,
            PerfectSwap = _G.SpecialsState.PerfectSwap,
            YutaBF = _G.SpecialsState.YutaBF,
            AutoM1Enabled = _G.SpecialsState.AutoM1Enabled
        },
        MiscState = {
            IsInvisible = _G.MiscState.IsInvisible,
            NoclipDomain = _G.MiscState.NoclipDomain,
            InfiniteParkour = _G.MiscState.InfiniteParkour,
            InfiniteDashActive = _G.MiscState.InfiniteDashActive,
            ACBypassEnabled = _G.MiscState.ACBypassEnabled,
            RapidTPEnabled = _G.MiscState.RapidTPEnabled
        },
        MiscStateFlight = {
            IsFlying = _G.MiscState.Flight.IsFlying,
            BaseSpeed = _G.MiscState.Flight.BaseSpeed,
            SprintSpeed = _G.MiscState.Flight.SprintSpeed,
            MaxFOV = _G.MiscState.Flight.MaxFOV,
            Keybind = typeof(_G.MiscState.Flight.Keybind) == "EnumItem" and _G.MiscState.Flight.Keybind.Name or _G.MiscState.Flight.Keybind
        },
        PianoState = {
            Speed = _G.PianoState.Speed,
            Transpose = _G.PianoState.Transpose,
            Looping = _G.PianoState.Looping
        },
        CharState = {
            SilentAnim = _G.CharState.SilentAnim,
            AntiRagdoll = _G.CharState.AntiRagdoll,
            HitboxExpander = _G.CharState.HitboxExpander,
            HitboxSize = _G.CharState.HitboxSize,
            AntiStun = _G.CharState.AntiStun,
            AntiStunTarget = _G.CharState.AntiStunTarget
        }
    }
    local success, str = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(configData)
    end)
    if success then
        writefile("SosyHUB/" .. name .. ".json", str)
        create_notification({ name = "Saved config: " .. name, duration = 2 })
    else
        create_notification({ name = "Error saving config!", duration = 2 })
    end
end

local UIElements = {}
local function SetElementValue(name, val)
    local elem = UIElements[name]
    if elem and val ~= nil then
        pcall(function()
            if elem.Set then elem:Set(val)
            elseif elem.SetValue then elem:SetValue(val) end
        end)
    end
end

local function loadConfig(name)
    if name == "" then
        create_notification({ name = "Select a config to load!", duration = 2 })
        return
    end
    local path = "SosyHUB/" .. name .. ".json"
    if not isfile(path) then
        create_notification({ name = "File not found!", duration = 2 })
        return
    end
    local success, data = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile(path))
    end)
    if success and type(data) == "table" then
        _G.IsLoadingConfig = true
        if data.BFSettings then
            if type(data.BFSettings.Range) == "number" then SetElementValue("Detection Range", data.BFSettings.Range) end
            if type(data.BFSettings.Radius) == "number" then SetElementValue("Behind Radius (Distance)", data.BFSettings.Radius) end
            if type(data.BFSettings.Duration) == "number" then SetElementValue("Dash Duration", math.floor(data.BFSettings.Duration * 100)) end
            if type(data.BFSettings.CamLift) == "number" then SetElementValue("Camera Lift", data.BFSettings.CamLift) end
            if type(data.BFSettings.Ms) == "number" then SetElementValue("Flash Timing (Ms)", data.BFSettings.Ms) end
            if type(data.BFSettings.Keybind) == "string" then SetElementValue("Dash Keybind", Enum.KeyCode[data.BFSettings.Keybind]) end
        end
        if data.AutoBlockState then SetElementValue("Enable Auto Block", data.AutoBlockState.Enabled) end
        if data.ABSettings then
            if type(data.ABSettings.MaxDistance) == "number" then SetElementValue("Trail Detection Distance", data.ABSettings.MaxDistance) end
            if type(data.ABSettings.BlockDuration) == "number" then SetElementValue("Block Duration (s)", data.ABSettings.BlockDuration) end
            if type(data.ABSettings.AheadThreshold) == "number" then SetElementValue("Trail Ahead Angle", math.deg(math.acos(math.clamp(data.ABSettings.AheadThreshold, -1, 1)))) end
            if type(data.ABSettings.MoveBlockEnabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Enable Move Anim Block", data.ABSettings.MoveBlockEnabled) end
            if type(data.ABSettings.MoveDetectionRange) == "number" then SetElementValue("Move Anim Detection Range", data.ABSettings.MoveDetectionRange) end
            if type(data.ABSettings.FacingAngleThreshold) == "number" then SetElementValue("Move Facing Sensitivity", data.ABSettings.FacingAngleThreshold) end
            if type(data.ABSettings.BlockWhileAttacking) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Block While Attacking", data.ABSettings.BlockWhileAttacking) end
            if type(data.ABSettings.ProjectileBlockEnabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Enable Projectile Block", data.ABSettings.ProjectileBlockEnabled) end
            if type(data.ABSettings.ProjectileRange) == "number" then SetElementValue("Projectile Detection Range", data.ABSettings.ProjectileRange) end
        end
        if data.LockSettings then
            if type(data.LockSettings.Sticky) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Sticky Target (Until Dead)", data.LockSettings.Sticky) end
            if type(data.LockSettings.Method) == "string" then SetElementValue("Method", data.LockSettings.Method) end
            if type(data.LockSettings.TargetMode) == "string" then SetElementValue("Target Mode", data.LockSettings.TargetMode) end
            if type(data.LockSettings.TargetPart) == "string" then SetElementValue("Target Part", data.LockSettings.TargetPart) end
            if type(data.LockSettings.SideOffset) == "number" then SetElementValue("Camera Side Offset", data.LockSettings.SideOffset) end
        end
        if data.LockState then SetElementValue("Enable Lock", data.LockState.Enabled) end
        if data.BlockBreakState then
            if type(data.BlockBreakState.Enabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Enable Block Break", data.BlockBreakState.Enabled) end
            if type(data.BlockBreakState.LockBehind) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Lock Behind Enemy", data.BlockBreakState.LockBehind) end
            if type(data.BlockBreakState.Distance) == "number" then SetElementValue("BB Detection Distance", data.BlockBreakState.Distance) end
            if type(data.BlockBreakState.Radius) == "number" then SetElementValue("Behind Radius", data.BlockBreakState.Radius) end
            if type(data.BlockBreakState.Duration) == "number" then SetElementValue("Dash Duration (s)", data.BlockBreakState.Duration) end
            if type(data.BlockBreakState.CurveStrength) == "number" then SetElementValue("BB Curve Strength", data.BlockBreakState.CurveStrength) end
        end
        if data.ESPState then
            if type(data.ESPState.ChamsEnabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Player Chams", data.ESPState.ChamsEnabled) end
            if type(data.ESPState.NameEnabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Player Name ESP", data.ESPState.NameEnabled) end
            if type(data.ESPState.CharacterEnabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Character ESP", data.ESPState.CharacterEnabled) end
            if type(data.ESPState.ItemsEnabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Item ESP", data.ESPState.ItemsEnabled) end
        end
        if data.SpecialsState then
            if type(data.SpecialsState.NanamiRatio) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Auto Ratio", data.SpecialsState.NanamiRatio) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.GarudaRebound) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Garuda Rebound", data.SpecialsState.GarudaRebound) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.GarudaDelay) == "number" then SetElementValue("Garuda Delay", data.SpecialsState.GarudaDelay) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.AutoWCS) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Auto WCS", data.SpecialsState.AutoWCS) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.NaoyaDashActive) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Naoya Infinite Front Dash", data.SpecialsState.NaoyaDashActive) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.HiguQTE) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Auto QTE (Final Judgement)", data.SpecialsState.HiguQTE) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.AutoDomain) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Auto Vote (Domain)", data.SpecialsState.AutoDomain) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.AutoDoorHakari) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Auto Door - Hakari", data.SpecialsState.AutoDoorHakari) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.FeverCrusher) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Fever Crusher", data.SpecialsState.FeverCrusher) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.AutoBodyRepel) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Auto Body Repel", data.SpecialsState.AutoBodyRepel) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.HeianSukunaBF) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Heian Sukuna Blackflash", data.SpecialsState.HeianSukunaBF) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.CleaveDelay) == "number" then SetElementValue("Heian Cleave Delay", data.SpecialsState.CleaveDelay) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.MahitoBF) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Mahito Blackflash", data.SpecialsState.MahitoBF) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.PerfectSwap) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Perfect Swap", data.SpecialsState.PerfectSwap) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.YutaBF) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Yuta Blackflash", data.SpecialsState.YutaBF) end
            if type(data.SpecialsState.AutoM1Enabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Auto M1 on R (Swap Detection)", data.SpecialsState.AutoM1Enabled) end
        end
        if data.CharState then
            if type(data.CharState.SilentAnim) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Silent Animations", data.CharState.SilentAnim) end
            if type(data.CharState.AntiRagdoll) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Anti-Ragdoll", data.CharState.AntiRagdoll) end
            if type(data.CharState.HitboxExpander) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Hitbox Expander", data.CharState.HitboxExpander) end
            if type(data.CharState.HitboxSize) == "number" then SetElementValue("Hitbox Size", data.CharState.HitboxSize) end
            if type(data.CharState.AntiStunTarget) == "string" then SetElementValue("Anti-Stun Target", data.CharState.AntiStunTarget) end
            if type(data.CharState.AntiStun) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Anti-Stun / Knockback", data.CharState.AntiStun) end
        end
        if data.MiscState then
            if type(data.MiscState.IsInvisible) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Invisible", data.MiscState.IsInvisible) end
            if type(data.MiscState.NoclipDomain) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Noclip Domain", data.MiscState.NoclipDomain) end
            if type(data.MiscState.InfiniteParkour) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Infinite Parkour", data.MiscState.InfiniteParkour) end
            if type(data.MiscState.InfiniteDashActive) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Infinite Dash", data.MiscState.InfiniteDashActive) end
            if type(data.MiscState.ACBypassEnabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("AC Bypass", data.MiscState.ACBypassEnabled) end
            if type(data.MiscState.RapidTPEnabled) == "boolean" then SetElementValue("Rapid TP", data.MiscState.RapidTPEnabled) end
        end
        if data.MiscStateFlight then
            if type(data.MiscStateFlight.BaseSpeed) == "number" then SetElementValue("Base Speed", data.MiscStateFlight.BaseSpeed) end
            if type(data.MiscStateFlight.SprintSpeed) == "number" then SetElementValue("Boost Speed (Shift)", data.MiscStateFlight.SprintSpeed) end
            if type(data.MiscStateFlight.MaxFOV) == "number" then SetElementValue("Max FOV", data.MiscStateFlight.MaxFOV) end
            if type(data.MiscStateFlight.Keybind) == "string" then SetElementValue("Flight Keybind", Enum.KeyCode[data.MiscStateFlight.Keybind]) end
        end
        if data.PianoState then
            if type(data.PianoState.Speed) == "number" then
                _G.PianoState.Speed = data.PianoState.Speed
                SetElementValue("Speed", math.floor(_G.PianoState.Speed * 100))
            end
            if type(data.PianoState.Transpose) == "number" then
                _G.PianoState.Transpose = data.PianoState.Transpose
                SetElementValue("Transpose (semitones)", _G.PianoState.Transpose)
            end
            if type(data.PianoState.Looping) == "boolean" then
                _G.PianoState.Looping = data.PianoState.Looping
                SetElementValue("Loop", _G.PianoState.Looping)
            end
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.2)
            _G.IsLoadingConfig = false
        end)
        create_notification({ name = "Successfully applied config: " .. name, duration = 3 })
    else
        create_notification({ name = "Error loading config!", duration = 2 })
    end
end

local function stopAltFarm()
    _G.AltFarmState.ismainfarming = false
    _G.AltFarmState.isaltfarming = false
    pcall(function()
        local HitboxController = require(lp.PlayerScripts.Controllers.Combat.HitboxController)
        if _G.AltFarmState.hitboxoriginal then
            HitboxController.SphereHitbox = _G.AltFarmState.hitboxoriginal
            _G.AltFarmState.hitboxoriginal = nil
        end
    end)
    if _G.AltFarmTPConn then
        _G.AltFarmTPConn:Disconnect()
        _G.AltFarmTPConn = nil
    end
end

local function startAltFarm()
    stopAltFarm()
    if _G.AltFarmState.farmtargetname == "" then
        create_notification({ name = "Select an Attacker first!", duration = 2 })
        return
    end
    _G.AltFarmState.isaltfarming = true
    _G.AltFarmState.farmalive = true
    local offset = CFrame.new(0, 0, -2.5)
    _G.AltFarmTPConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.AltFarmState.isaltfarming then return end
        local targetPlayer = Players:FindFirstChild(_G.AltFarmState.farmtargetname)
        local targetChar = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character
        local myChar = lp.Character
        if targetChar and myChar then
            pcall(function()
                local targetHRP = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if targetHRP and myHRP then
                    myChar:PivotTo(targetHRP.CFrame * offset)
                    myHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    myHRP.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.AltFarmState.isaltfarming do
            if _G.AltFarmState.farmalive and lp.Character then
                pcall(function()
                    if replicatesignal then replicatesignal(lp.Kill) end
                end)
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
    end)
end

local function startMainFarm()
    stopAltFarm()
    _G.AltFarmState.ismainfarming = true
    _G.AltFarmState.farmalive = true
    pcall(function()
        local HitboxController = require(lp.PlayerScripts.Controllers.Combat.HitboxController)
        if not _G.AltFarmState.hitboxoriginal then
            _G.AltFarmState.hitboxoriginal = HitboxController.SphereHitbox
            HitboxController.SphereHitbox = function(self, p1, p2, p3, p4, ...)
                return _G.AltFarmState.hitboxoriginal(self, p1, p2, p3, 60, ...)
            end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.AltFarmState.ismainfarming do
            pcall(function()
                local char = lp.Character
                if char then
                    local moveset = char:GetAttribute("Moveset")
                    if moveset then
                        local serviceName = moveset .. "Service"
                        local Knit = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Knit.Knit)
                        local service = Knit.Services:FindFirstChild(serviceName)
                        if service and service.RE and service.RE.Activated then
                            service.RE.Activated:FireServer(false)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.12)
        end
    end)
end

local autoDomainConn
local function startAutoDomain()
    if autoDomainConn then autoDomainConn:Disconnect() end
    local voting = false
    local lastTick = 0
    autoDomainConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.SpecialsState.AutoDomain then return end
        if tick() - lastTick < 0.2 then return end
        lastTick = tick()
        local playerGui = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not playerGui then return end
        local choose = playerGui:FindFirstChild("Choose")
        if not choose then return end
        local timer = choose:FindFirstChild("Timer")
        if not timer or timer.Text ~= "1" then return end
        if voting then return end
        local domains = workspace:FindFirstChild("Domains")
        if not domains then return end
        local domain = domains:FindFirstChild("Domain")
        if not domain then return end
        if domain:GetAttribute("UI2") == true then return end
        local confessCount = domain:GetAttribute("ConfessCount") or 0
        local denialCount = domain:GetAttribute("DenialCount") or 0
        local silenceCount = domain:GetAttribute("SilenceCount") or 0
        local choice = "Silence"
        local maxCount = silenceCount
        if confessCount > maxCount then maxCount = confessCount; choice = "Confess" end
        if denialCount > maxCount then maxCount = denialCount; choice = "Denial" end
        local btn = choose:FindFirstChild(choice)
        if btn then
            voting = true
            pcall(function()
                if getconnections then
                    for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(btn.MouseButton1Down)) do conn:Fire() end
                    for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(btn.Activated)) do conn:Fire() end
                    for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(btn.MouseButton1Click)) do conn:Fire() end
                end
            end)
            task.delay(1, function() voting = false end)
        end
    end)
end

local function stopAutoDomain()
    if autoDomainConn then
        autoDomainConn:Disconnect()
        autoDomainConn = nil
    end
end

local function getPlayersList()
    local list = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= lp then table.insert(list, p.Name) end
    end
    table.sort(list)
    return list
end

local function r148(v)
    _G.SpecialsState.NanamiRatio = v
    if _G.SpecialsState.AutoRatioConn then
        _G.SpecialsState.AutoRatioConn:Disconnect()
        _G.SpecialsState.AutoRatioConn = nil
    end
    if not v then return end
    pcall(function()
        local effects = game.ReplicatedStorage.Knit.Knit.Services.NanamiService.RE.Effects
        local rightActivated = game.ReplicatedStorage.Knit.Knit.Services.NanamiService.RE.RightActivated
        _G.SpecialsState.AutoRatioConn = effects.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(...)
            local args = {...}
            if args[1] == "SpawnRatio" and args[2] == lp then
                local waitTime = (args[6] * 0.56767676767676769420) - lp:GetNetworkPing()
                if waitTime > 0 then task.wait(waitTime) end
                pcall(function() rightActivated:FireServer() end)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

local function r150(v)
    _G.SpecialsState.NaoyaDashActive = v
    if v then
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, MovementController = pcall(function()
                return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Controllers.Character.MovementController)
            end)
            if not ok or not MovementController then return end
            while _G.SpecialsState.NaoyaDashActive do
                local char = lp.Character
                if char then
                    pcall(function() MovementController:Dash(lp, char, "Front") end)
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function r153(v)
    _G.SpecialsState.HiguQTE = v
    if _G.SpecialsState.AutoQTEConn then
        _G.SpecialsState.AutoQTEConn:Disconnect()
        _G.SpecialsState.AutoQTEConn = nil
    end
    if not v then return end
    local lastTick = 0
    local lastPressed = 0
    _G.SpecialsState.AutoQTEConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if tick() - lastTick < 0.1 then return end
        lastTick = tick()
        local playerGui = lp:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not playerGui then return end
        local qte = playerGui:FindFirstChild("QTE")
        if qte then
            local pc = qte:FindFirstChild("QTE_PC")
            if pc and pc:IsA("TextLabel") then
                local keyStr = string.match(string.upper(pc.Text), "[A-Z]")
                if keyStr and tick() - lastPressed > 0.2 then
                    lastPressed = tick()
                    local keyCode = Enum.KeyCode[keyStr]
                    if keyCode then
                        task.spawn(function()
                            local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, keyCode, false, game)
                            task.wait(0.03)
                            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, keyCode, false, game)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function createToggle(section, tbl)
    local element = section:Toggle(tbl)
    UIElements[tbl.Name] = element
    return element
end

local function createSlider(section, tbl)
    local element = section:Slider(tbl)
    UIElements[tbl.Name] = element
    return element
end

local function createDropdown(section, tbl)
    local element = section:Dropdown(tbl)
    UIElements[tbl.Name] = element
    return element
end

local function createKeybind(section, tbl)
    local element = section:Keybind(tbl)
    UIElements[tbl.Name] = element
    return element
end

local function createTextbox(section, tbl)
    local element = section:Input(tbl)
    UIElements[tbl.Name] = element
    return element
end

local function createButton(section, tbl)
    local element = section:Button(tbl)
    UIElements[tbl.Name] = element
    return element
end

    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.MiscState.NoclipDomain = v
        if v then
            _G.NoclipConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not _G.MiscState.NoclipDomain then return end
                local domains = workspace:FindFirstChild("Domains")
                if domains then
                    local d = domains:FindFirstChild("Domain")
                    if d and d:IsA("BasePart") then d.CanCollide = false end
                end
            end)
        else
            if _G.NoclipConn then _G.NoclipConn:Disconnect(); _G.NoclipConn = nil end
        end
    end
})

createToggle(MoveModSection, {
    Name = "Infinite Parkour",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.MiscState.InfiniteParkour = v
        if v then
            local ok, mc = pcall(function()
                return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Controllers.Character.MovementController)
            end)
            if not ok or not mc or not mc.Parkour then return end
            _G.ParkourConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not _G.MiscState.InfiniteParkour then return end
                pcall(function()
                    for k, val in pairs(debug.getupvalues(mc.Parkour)) do
                        if type(val) == "number" then debug.setupvalue(mc.Parkour, k, 0) end
                    end
                end)
            end)
        else
            if _G.ParkourConn then _G.ParkourConn:Disconnect(); _G.ParkourConn = nil end
        end
    end
})

createToggle(MoveModSection, {
    Name = "Infinite Dash",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.MiscState.InfiniteDashActive = v
        if not hookfunction then return end
        if v then
            _G.MiscState.FakeTime = _G.MiscState.OriginalTick()
            hookfunction(tick, function()
                _G.MiscState.FakeTime = _G.MiscState.FakeTime + 100
                return _G.MiscState.FakeTime
            end)
        else
            hookfunction(tick, _G.MiscState.OriginalTick)
        end
    end
})

createToggle(MoveModSection, {
    Name = "Invisible",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.MiscState.IsInvisible = v
        local function GetCharacterParts()
            local char = lp.Character
            if not char then return nil, nil, nil, nil end
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            return char, hrp, hum, animator
        end
        local function DisconnectInvisConns()
            if _G.MiscState.InvisConnections then
                for name, conn in pairs(_G.MiscState.InvisConnections) do
                    if conn and typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then conn:Disconnect() end
                end
                _G.MiscState.InvisConnections = {}
            end
        end
        if v then
            local char, hrp, hum, animator = GetCharacterParts()
            if not char or not hrp or not hum or not animator then
                create_notification({ name = "No valid character found", duration = 3 })
                return
            end
            _G.MiscState.InvisOriginalCameraSubject = Camera.CameraSubject
            _G.MiscState.InvisConnections = {}
            hum.AutoRotate = false
            hrp.Anchored = true
            _G.MiscState.InvisConnections.Noclip = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
                if not _G.MiscState.IsInvisible then return end
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end
                end
            end)
            local torso = Instance.new("Part")
            torso.Name = "InvisibleFakeTorso"
            torso.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
            torso.Transparency = 1
            torso.CanCollide = false
            torso.Anchored = true
            torso.Parent = workspace
            _G.MiscState.InvisibleFakeTorso = torso
            Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            Camera.CameraSubject = torso
            RunService:BindToRenderStep("InvisCameraSync", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                if not _G.MiscState.IsInvisible or not hrp or not hrp.Parent or not torso then return end
                local camCF = Camera.CFrame
                local lookDir = Vector3.new(camCF.LookVector.X, 0, camCF.LookVector.Z)
                if lookDir.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + lookDir.Unit)
                end
                torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0))
            end)
            local meditationAnim = RS:WaitForChild("Modules", 5)
                and RS.Modules:WaitForChild("MVP", 5)
                and RS.Modules.MVP:WaitForChild("Meditation", 5)
                and RS.Modules.MVP.Meditation:FindFirstChild("Character")
            if meditationAnim then
                local track = animator:LoadAnimation(meditationAnim)
                track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
                track:Play()
                task.wait(0.1)
                track.TimePosition = 0.1
                track:AdjustSpeed(0)
                _G.MiscState.InvisMeditationTrack = track
            end
            if animator then
                for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if track ~= _G.MiscState.InvisMeditationTrack then track:Stop(0) end
                end
                _G.MiscState.InvisConnections.AnimSuppress = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(playedTrack)
                    if playedTrack ~= _G.MiscState.InvisMeditationTrack then playedTrack:Stop(0) end
                end)
            end
        else
            DisconnectInvisConns()
            pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("InvisCameraSync") end)
            local char, hrp, hum = GetCharacterParts()
            if char then
                for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = true end
                end
            end
            if hrp then hrp.Anchored = false end
            if hum then
                hum.AutoRotate = true
                Camera.CameraSubject = hum
            elseif _G.MiscState.InvisOriginalCameraSubject then
                Camera.CameraSubject = _G.MiscState.InvisOriginalCameraSubject
            end
            if _G.MiscState.InvisibleFakeTorso then
                pcall(function() _G.MiscState.InvisibleFakeTorso:Destroy() end)
                _G.MiscState.InvisibleFakeTorso = nil
            end
            if _G.MiscState.InvisMeditationTrack then
                pcall(function() _G.MiscState.InvisMeditationTrack:Stop(0) end)
                pcall(function() _G.MiscState.InvisMeditationTrack:Destroy() end)
                _G.MiscState.InvisMeditationTrack = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

local TeleportSection = TeleportsTab:Section({ Name = "Teleport Locations", Side = "Left" })

createToggle(SpecialsSection, {
    Name = "Higuruma Vote Viewer",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            local old = game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("HigurumaVoteViewerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):FindFirstChild("HigurumaVoteViewerGui")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            sg.Name = "HigurumaVoteViewerGui"
            sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
            local success, err = pcall(function() sg.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
            if not success then sg.Parent = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
            local main = Instance.new("Frame")
            main.Name = "MainFrame"
            main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 250, 0, 44)
            main.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -125, 0.1, 0)
            main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 20)
            main.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            main.BorderSizePixel = 0
            main.Active = true
            main.Draggable = true
            main.Parent = sg
            local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
            corner.Parent = main
            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(218, 165, 32)
            stroke.Thickness = 1.2
            stroke.Transparency = 0.3
            stroke.Parent = main
            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Text = "COURT VOTES"
            title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(218, 165, 32)
            title.TextSize = 12
            title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            title.Parent = main
            local votesLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            votesLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
            votesLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
            votesLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            votesLabel.Text = "Confess: 0  |  Denial: 0  |  Silence: 0"
            votesLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 245)
            votesLabel.TextSize = 11
            votesLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
            votesLabel.Parent = main
            local function updateUI(domain)
                if not domain then votesLabel.Text = "Waiting for domain..."; return end
                local confess = domain:GetAttribute("ConfessCount") or 0
                local denial = domain:GetAttribute("DenialCount") or 0
                local silence = domain:GetAttribute("SilenceCount") or 0
                votesLabel.Text = string.format("Confess: %d  |  Denial: %d  |  Silence: %d", confess, denial, silence)
            end
            local lastDomain = nil
            local attributeConn = nil
            local function hookDomain(domain)
                if attributeConn then attributeConn:Disconnect(); attributeConn = nil end
                if domain then
                    updateUI(domain)
                    attributeConn = domain.AttributeChanged:Connect(function() updateUI(domain) end)
                else
                    updateUI(nil)
                end
            end
            local monitorConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local domains = workspace:FindFirstChild("Domains")
                local domain = domains and domains:FindFirstChild("Domain")
                if domain ~= lastDomain then lastDomain = domain; hookDomain(domain) end
            end)
            sg.Destroying:Connect(function()
                if attributeConn then attributeConn:Disconnect() end
                if monitorConn then monitorConn:Disconnect() end
            end)
        else
            local old = game:GetService("CoreGui"):FindFirstChild("HigurumaVoteViewerGui") or lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):FindFirstChild("HigurumaVoteViewerGui")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end
    end
})

local PianoMainSection = PianoTab:Section({ Name = "MIDI Player", Side = "Left" })

local pianoSongNames, pianoSongMap = pianoScanSongs()
_G.PianoState.FileMap = pianoSongMap

local pianoSongDropdown = createDropdown(PianoMainSection, {
    Name = "Song",
    Default = 1,
    Options = pianoSongNames,
    Callback = function(o)
        local p = _G.PianoState.FileMap[o]
        if p then
            local ok, data = pcall(readfile, p)
            if ok then
                local parsed = pianoParseMidi(data)
                if #parsed > 0 then
                    pianoStopPlayback()
                    _G.PianoState.FilePath = p
                    _G.PianoState.Song = parsed
                    _G.PianoState.TotalDuration = parsed[#parsed] and parsed[#parsed].time or 0
                    _G.PianoState.CurrentTime = 0
                    _G.PianoState.Index = 1
                    _G.PianoState.LastTime = 0
                end
            end
        end
    end,
})

PianoMainSection:Button({
    Name = "Refresh Songs",
    Callback = function()
        local names, map = pianoScanSongs()
        _G.PianoState.FileMap = map
        pcall(function() pianoSongDropdown:set_items(names) end)
        create_notification({ name = "Song list refreshed!", duration = 2 })
    end
})

    Name = "Speed",
    Minimum = 25, Maximum = 300, Default = 100,

})

local _pianoSliderUpdating = false
local pianoProgressSlider = createSlider(PianoMainSection, {
    Name = "Progress",
    Minimum = 0, Maximum = 100, Default = 0,
    DisplayMethod = "Value",
    Callback = function(v)
        if _pianoSliderUpdating then return end
        if _G.PianoState.Status == "Playing" then return end
        local total = _G.PianoState.TotalDuration
        if total > 0 then pianoSeek((v / 100) * total) end
    end,
})

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        local total = _G.PianoState.TotalDuration
        local current = _G.PianoState.CurrentTime
        if total > 0 then
            local percent = math.clamp((current / total) * 100, 0, 100)
            _pianoSliderUpdating = true
            pcall(function() pianoProgressSlider:SetValue(percent) end)
            _pianoSliderUpdating = false
        end
    end
end)

local pianoTimeLabel = createTextbox(PianoMainSection, {
    Name = "Time",
    Placeholder = "0:00 / 0:00",
    Callback = function() end,
})

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(0.1)
        local total = _G.PianoState.TotalDuration
        local current = _G.PianoState.CurrentTime
        if total > 0 then
            local min = math.floor(current / 60)
            local sec = math.floor(current % 60)
            local tmin = math.floor(total / 60)
            local tsec = math.floor(total % 60)
            local str = string.format("%d:%02d / %d:%02d", min, sec, tmin, tsec)
            pcall(function() pianoTimeLabel:SetValue(str) end)
        else
            pcall(function() pianoTimeLabel:SetValue("0:00 / 0:00") end)
        end
    end
end)

-- USER PROVIDED SIDE DASH SCRIPT --
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

local LEFT_DASH_ANIM = "rbxassetid://75203303352791"
local RIGHT_DASH_ANIM = "rbxassetid://117223862448096"

local sideDashInProgress = false
local lastSideDashEnd = 0

local SideDashKeybind = Enum.KeyCode.Q
local SideDashRange = function() return _G.OriginalSideDashRange or 20 end
local SideDashDistance = 3.0
local SideDashDuration = function() return _G.OriginalSideDashDuration or 0.35 end
local SideDashArcWidth = 8
local SideDashCamLift = 3.0

local function moveCharTo(char, cf)
    pcall(function()
        char:PivotTo(cf)
    end)
end

local function getSideDashTarget(maxRange)
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not myChar then return nil end
    local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myHRP then return nil end

    local nearest = nil
    local nearestDist = maxRange

    for _, plr in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
            local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                if not plr.Character:GetAttribute("Dead") then
                    local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < nearestDist then
                        nearestDist = dist
                        nearest = plr.Character
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local charsFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
    if charsFolder then
        for _, char in pairs(charsFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if char:IsA("Model") and char ~= myChar then
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    if not char:GetAttribute("Dead") then
                        local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < nearestDist then
                            nearestDist = dist
                            nearest = char
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return nearest
end

local function startSideDash()
    if sideDashInProgress then return end
    if tick() - lastSideDashEnd < 0.5 then return end

    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not myChar then return end
    local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myHRP then return end

    local hum = myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if myChar:GetAttribute("Dead") then return end

    local ragdoll = myChar:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")
    if ragdoll and ragdoll > 0 then return end

    local target = getSideDashTarget(SideDashRange())
    if not target then return end

    local targetHum = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if targetHum and targetHum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then return end
    if target:GetAttribute("Stun") then return end
    if target:GetAttribute("Ragdolled") then return end
    local targetRagdoll = target:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")
    if targetRagdoll and targetRagdoll > 0 then return end

    local tHRP = target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not tHRP or not tHRP.Parent then return end

    sideDashInProgress = true

    local startTime = tick()
    local myY = myHRP.Position.Y

    local myStartPos = Vector3.new(myHRP.Position.X, 0, myHRP.Position.Z)
    local targetPos = Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, 0, tHRP.Position.Z)

    local targetRightVec = Vector3.new(
        tHRP.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, tHRP.CFrame.RightVector.Z
    ).Unit

    local targetLookVec = Vector3.new(
        tHRP.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, tHRP.CFrame.LookVector.Z
    ).Unit

    local toPlayer = myStartPos - targetPos
    local sideSign = toPlayer:Dot(targetRightVec) >= 0 and 1 or -1

    local sidePos = targetPos + (targetRightVec * SideDashArcWidth * sideSign)
    local behindPos = targetPos - (targetLookVec * SideDashDistance)

    local animId = sideSign > 0 and RIGHT_DASH_ANIM or LEFT_DASH_ANIM

    local dist = toPlayer.Magnitude
    local speedMultiplier = math.clamp(dist / 20, 0.3, 3)
    local dynamicDuration = SideDashDuration() * speedMultiplier
    local animSpeed = 1 / speedMultiplier

    local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    local dashTrack = nil
    if animator then
        pcall(function()
            local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
            anim.AnimationId = animId
            dashTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
            dashTrack:Play(0.1, 1, animSpeed)
        end)
    end

    local phase1Time = dynamicDuration * 0.45
    local phase2Time = dynamicDuration * 0.55

    hum.AutoRotate = false
    local prevCam = Camera.CameraType
    Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom

    local dashTimeout = dynamicDuration + 1.5

    local conn
    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local elapsed = tick() - startTime

        local function abort()
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
            pcall(function()
                if hum and hum.Parent then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            end)
            pcall(function() Camera.CameraType = prevCam end)
            pcall(function()
                if dashTrack then dashTrack:Stop() end
            end)
            sideDashInProgress = false
            lastSideDashEnd = tick()
        end

        if elapsed > dashTimeout then abort() return end
        if not target or not target.Parent then abort() return end

        local targetHum = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not targetHum or targetHum.Health <= 0 then abort() return end
        if target:GetAttribute("Dead") then abort() return end

        tHRP = target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not tHRP or not tHRP.Parent then abort() return end

        myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myHRP or not myHRP.Parent then abort() return end
        if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then abort() return end

        local currentY = myHRP.Position.Y
        local currentPos
        local lookTarget

        if elapsed < phase1Time then
            local alpha = math.clamp(elapsed / phase1Time, 0, 1)
            local eased = 1 - (1 - alpha) ^ 2
            currentPos = myStartPos:Lerp(sidePos, eased)
            lookTarget = Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, currentY, tHRP.Position.Z)

            pcall(function()
                Camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                    myHRP.Position + Vector3.new(0, SideDashCamLift * alpha, 0),
                    tHRP.Position
                )
            end)
        else
            local phase2Elapsed = elapsed - phase1Time
            local alpha = math.clamp(phase2Elapsed / phase2Time, 0, 1)
            local eased = alpha < 0.5
                and 2 * alpha * alpha
                or 1 - (-2 * alpha + 2) ^ 2 / 2
            currentPos = sidePos:Lerp(behindPos, eased)
            lookTarget = Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, currentY, tHRP.Position.Z)

            pcall(function()
                Camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                    myHRP.Position + Vector3.new(0, SideDashCamLift * (1 - alpha), 0),
                    tHRP.Position
                )
            end)
        end

        if currentPos then
            moveCharTo(myChar, CFrame.new(
                Vector3.new(currentPos.X, currentY, currentPos.Z),
                lookTarget
            ))
        end

        if elapsed >= SideDashDuration() then
            pcall(function()
                if myHRP and myHRP.Parent and tHRP and tHRP.Parent then
                    local finalY = myHRP.Position.Y
                    moveCharTo(myChar, CFrame.new(
                        Vector3.new(behindPos.X, finalY, behindPos.Z),
                        Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, finalY, tHRP.Position.Z)
                    ))
                end
            end)
            abort()
        end
    end)
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed then return end
    if not _G.OriginalSideDashEnabled then return end
    if input.KeyCode == SideDashKeybind then
        startSideDash()
    end
end)

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    sideDashInProgress = false
    lastSideDashEnd = 0
end)

end

do -- USER PROVIDED BFC SCRIPT --
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

local function isScriptEnabled() return _G.NewBFCEnabled end
local dashing = false

local Settings = {
    Duration = function() return _G.NewBFCDuration or 0.35 end,
    StopDistance = function() return _G.NewBFCStopDistance or 3 end,
    Range = function() return _G.NewBFCRange or 25 end,
    CurveStrength = function() return _G.NewBFCCurveStrength or 8 end,
    CamLift = function() return _G.NewBFCCamLift or 4 end,
}

local AnimationTriggers = {
    ["rbxassetid://100962226150441"] = 0.19,
    ["rbxassetid://95852624447551"] = 0.19,
    ["rbxassetid://74145636023952"] = 0.19,
    ["rbxassetid://72475960800126"] = 0.20,
}

local cachedRE = nil
local function getActivatedRE()
    if cachedRE and cachedRE.Parent then return cachedRE end

    local paths = {
        function()
            return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Knit", 3)
                :WaitForChild("Knit", 3)
                :WaitForChild("Services", 3)
                :WaitForChild("DivergentFistService", 3)
                :WaitForChild("RE", 3)
                :WaitForChild("Activated", 3)
        end,
        function()
            return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Knit", 3)
                :WaitForChild("Services", 3)
                :WaitForChild("DivergentFistService", 3)
                :WaitForChild("RE", 3)
                :WaitForChild("Activated", 3)
        end,
    }

    for _, pathFunc in ipairs(paths) do
        local ok, re = pcall(pathFunc)
        if ok and re then
            cachedRE = re
            return re
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function fireBlackFlash()
    if not isScriptEnabled() then return end
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local moveset = char:FindFirstChild("Moveset")
    if not moveset then return end
    local move = moveset:FindFirstChild("Divergent Fist")
    if not move then return end
    local re = getActivatedRE()
    if not re then return end
    re:FireServer(move)
end

local function getHRP(character)
    return character and (
        character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or
        character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or
        character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
    )
end

local function getNearestEnemy(maxRange, exclude)
    local myHRP = getHRP(LocalPlayer.Character)
    if not myHRP then return nil end
    local nearest, nearestDist = nil, maxRange

    local function consider(charModel, ownerPlayer)
        if charModel == LocalPlayer.Character then return end
        if exclude and ownerPlayer == exclude then return end
        local hum = charModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local hrp = getHRP(charModel)
        if hum and hum.Health > 0 and hrp then
            local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
            if dist < nearestDist then
                nearestDist = dist
                nearest = ownerPlayer or { Character = charModel }
            end
        end
    end

    for _, pl in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if pl ~= LocalPlayer and pl.Character then
            consider(pl.Character, pl)
        end
    end

    local charFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Characters")
    if charFolder then
        for _, obj in ipairs(charFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("Model") then consider(obj, nil) end
        end
    else
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj) then
                consider(obj, nil)
            end
        end
    end

    return nearest
end

local function isEnemyRagdolled(target)
    local char = target and target.Character or target
    if not char then return false end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = getHRP(char)
    if hum and hrp then
        return hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or math.abs(hrp.CFrame.UpVector.Y) < 0.7
    end
    return false
end

local function startDash()
    if not isScriptEnabled() then return end
    dashing = true

    local target = getNearestEnemy(Settings.Range())
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myHRP = getHRP(myChar)
    local hum = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not (target and myHRP and hum) then dashing = false return end
    if isEnemyRagdolled(target) then dashing = false return end

    local targetChar = target.Character or (typeof(target) == "Instance" and target)
    if not targetChar then dashing = false return end
    local tHRP = getHRP(targetChar)
    if not tHRP or not tHRP.Parent then dashing = false return end

    local startTime = tick()
    local startPos = myHRP.Position
    local initTargetCF = tHRP.CFrame
    local initLookFlat = Vector3.new(initTargetCF.LookVector.X, 0, initTargetCF.LookVector.Z).Unit
    local initRightVec = Vector3.new(initTargetCF.RightVector.X, 0, initTargetCF.RightVector.Z).Unit
    local toPlayer = Vector3.new(startPos.X, 0, startPos.Z) - Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, 0, tHRP.Position.Z)
    local sideSign = toPlayer:Dot(initRightVec) >= 0 and 1 or -1

    local startY = startPos.Y

    local function getBehindPos()
        if not (tHRP and tHRP.Parent) then return nil end
        return tHRP.Position - initLookFlat * Settings.StopDistance()
    end

    local function getControlPoint()
        if not (tHRP and tHRP.Parent) then return nil end
        local flat = Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, 0, tHRP.Position.Z)
        local ctrl = flat + initRightVec * (Settings.CurveStrength() * sideSign)
        return Vector3.new(ctrl.X, startY, ctrl.Z)
    end

    hum.AutoRotate = false
    local prevCamType = Camera.CameraType
    Camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom

    local rawEndInitial = getBehindPos()
    local smoothedEnd = rawEndInitial or startPos
    local lastTime = tick()
    local timeout = Settings.Duration() + 1.5
    local flashFired = false

    local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
    bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    bodyVelocity.Parent = myHRP

    local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e5, 1e5, 1e5)
    bodyGyro.D = 100
    bodyGyro.P = 10000
    bodyGyro.CFrame = myHRP.CFrame
    bodyGyro.Parent = myHRP

    local conn
    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        local now = tick()

        if now - startTime > timeout or not ScriptEnabled then
            conn:Disconnect()
            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
            bodyGyro:Destroy()
            if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            Camera.CameraType = prevCamType
            dashing = false
            return
        end

        myHRP = getHRP(LocalPlayer.Character)
        if not (myHRP and myHRP.Parent and hum.Health > 0) then
            conn:Disconnect()
            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
            bodyGyro:Destroy()
            if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            Camera.CameraType = prevCamType
            dashing = false
            return
        end

        local curTargetChar = target.Character or (typeof(target) == "Instance" and target)
        local curHum = curTargetChar and curTargetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not curTargetChar or not curHum or curHum.Health <= 0 then
            local newTarget = getNearestEnemy(Settings.Range(), target)
            if newTarget then
                target = newTarget
                curTargetChar = target.Character or (typeof(target) == "Instance" and target)
                if curTargetChar then
                    tHRP = getHRP(curTargetChar)
                    if tHRP then
                        initTargetCF = tHRP.CFrame
                        initLookFlat = Vector3.new(initTargetCF.LookVector.X, 0, initTargetCF.LookVector.Z).Unit
                        initRightVec = Vector3.new(initTargetCF.RightVector.X, 0, initTargetCF.RightVector.Z).Unit
                        local curPos = Vector3.new(myHRP.Position.X, 0, myHRP.Position.Z)
                        local tgtPos = Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, 0, tHRP.Position.Z)
                        sideSign = (curPos - tgtPos):Dot(initRightVec) >= 0 and 1 or -1
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if not (tHRP and tHRP.Parent) then
            conn:Disconnect()
            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
            bodyGyro:Destroy()
            if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            Camera.CameraType = prevCamType
            dashing = false
            return
        end

        lastTime = now
        local alpha = math.clamp((now - startTime) / Settings.Duration(), 0, 1)

        if alpha >= 1 then
            conn:Disconnect()
            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
            bodyGyro:Destroy()
            if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            Camera.CameraType = prevCamType
            local finalPos = getBehindPos()
            if finalPos then
                myHRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                    Vector3.new(finalPos.X, myHRP.Position.Y, finalPos.Z),
                    Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, myHRP.Position.Y, tHRP.Position.Z)
                )
            end
            dashing = false
            return
        end

        local rawEnd = getBehindPos()
        if not rawEnd then
            conn:Disconnect()
            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
            bodyGyro:Destroy()
            if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            Camera.CameraType = prevCamType
            dashing = false
            return
        end

        local MAX_DEST_SPD = 18
        local delta = rawEnd - smoothedEnd
        local maxStep = MAX_DEST_SPD * dt
        if delta.Magnitude > maxStep then
            smoothedEnd = smoothedEnd + delta.Unit * maxStep
        else
            smoothedEnd = rawEnd
        end

        local ctrlPt = getControlPoint()
        if not ctrlPt then
            conn:Disconnect()
            bodyVelocity:Destroy()
            bodyGyro:Destroy()
            if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            Camera.CameraType = prevCamType
            dashing = false
            return
        end

        local ease = 1 - (1 - alpha) ^ 2
        local e1 = 1 - ease

        local blendedCtrl = Vector3.new(
            ctrlPt.X * (1 - alpha) + smoothedEnd.X * alpha,
            ctrlPt.Y,
            ctrlPt.Z * (1 - alpha) + smoothedEnd.Z * alpha
        )

        local targetPos = Vector3.new(
            (e1 * e1) * startPos.X + (2 * e1 * ease) * blendedCtrl.X + (ease * ease) * smoothedEnd.X,
            myHRP.Position.Y,
            (e1 * e1) * startPos.Z + (2 * e1 * ease) * blendedCtrl.Z + (ease * ease) * smoothedEnd.Z
        )

        local diff = targetPos - myHRP.Position
        if dt > 0 then
            bodyVelocity.Velocity = diff / dt
        end

        local lookTarget = Vector3.new(tHRP.Position.X, myHRP.Position.Y, tHRP.Position.Z)
        bodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.new(myHRP.Position, lookTarget)

        Camera.CFrame = CFrame.new(
            myHRP.Position + Vector3.new(0, Settings.CamLift(), 0),
            tHRP.Position
        )
    end)
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Three then
        startDash()
    end
end)

local function setupCharacter(character)
    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    local animator = humanoid and humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
    if not animator then return end

    animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
        if not isScriptEnabled() then return end
        local animId = track.Animation.AnimationId
        local delayTime = AnimationTriggers[animId]
        if delayTime then
            task.delay(delayTime, function()
                if not (humanoid.Health > 0 and isScriptEnabled()) then return end
                fireBlackFlash()
            end)
        end
    end)

end

if LocalPlayer.Character then setupCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character) end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupCharacter)
end

do -- BFC (1) SCRIPT --
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

local function isScriptEnabled() return _G.BFC1Enabled end
local dashing = false

local Settings = {
    Duration = 0.35,
    StopDistance = 3,
    Range = 25,
    CurveStrength = 8,
    CamLift = 4,
}

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.E then
        startDash()
    end
end)

local function setupCharacter(character)
    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    local animator = humanoid and humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
    if not animator then return end

    
        end
    end)
end

if LocalPlayer.Character then setupCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character) end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupCharacter)
end
